- **data-storage**: Implement client-side data storage with localStorage, IndexedDB, or SQLite WASM. Use when storing user preferences, caching data, or building offline-first applications. - **data-storage**: Guide for choosing and using data storage on Borg HPC, including scratch space, shared filesystems, quotas, temporary data, and moving data efficiently. Use this skill whenever a user asks where to store files, how to use scratch, how to move data onto or off Borg, how to avoid quota or filesystem issues, or why a job is slow due to I/O.
